# HalloHome — Restore Snapshot

Total files: 48

## `.github/workflows/verify.yml`

```yaml
name: Verify

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]

jobs:
  verify:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install pnpm
        uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4
        with:
          version: 9

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 20
          cache: pnpm

      - name: Setup Rust
        uses: dtolnay/rust-toolchain@stable
        with:
          components: rustfmt, clippy

      - name: Rust cache
        uses: Swatinem/rust-cache@v2

      - name: Install Linux system deps (Tauri v2)
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y \
            libgtk-3-dev \
            libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev \
            libappindicator3-dev \
            librsvg2-dev \
            patchelf

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: pnpm install --no-frozen-lockfile

      - name: Verify
        run: pnpm verify
```

*~

tmp/
temp/

# Testing
coverage/

src/ui/public/brand/
src/tauri/icons/
```

## `Cargo.toml`

```toml
[workspace]
resolver = "2"
members = ["src/core", "src/tauri"]

[workspace.package]
version = "0.0.2"
edition = "2021"
license = "GPL-3.0-or-later"
authors = ["Rama Eka Yudha"]
repository = "https://github.com/ramaekayudha/HalloHome"
rust-version = "1.78"

[workspace.dependencies]
hallohome-core = { path = "src/core" }
serde = { version = "1", features = ["derive"] }
serde_json = "1"
```

## `clippy.toml`

```toml
avoid-breaking-exported-api = false
```

## `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`

```markdown
# HalloHome — Architecture

## Layer separation (permanent rule)

| Layer | Path | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Core engine | `src/core` | Rust, UI-agnostic. Never imports from `src/ui` or `src/tauri`. |
| Bridge | `src/tauri` | Tauri v2. Calls `hallohome-core` and exposes commands. |
| UI | `src/ui` | Svelte 5 + Vite + TypeScript. Calls Tauri commands only. |

`src/core` must compile independently:

```bash
cargo build -p hallohome-core
```

## Data flow

```
UI (Svelte)  ->  Tauri command  ->  Core engine  ->  Result  ->  UI
```

Rules:

- No business logic in the Tauri bridge.
- No UI concerns in the core engine.
- No cross-imports between layers except: `src/tauri` depends on `src/core`, and `src/ui` communicates through Tauri commands.

## File formats

- Project file: `.hallohome` (JSON, versioned via `SCHEMA_VERSION`).
- 3D asset: glTF 2.0 / GLB.

## Typography

Lora only. Bundled as `woff2` under `src/ui/public/fonts/`.

- No CDN.
- No additional fonts.
- `@font-face` declarations live in `src/ui/src/styles/lora.css`.

## Themes

`data-theme="light" | "dark"` on `<html>`.

- Light: Corporate Blue.
- Dark: Neon Orange.

CSS variables live in `src/ui/src/styles/theme.css`.

User selection persisted in `localStorage` under key `hallohome.theme`.

## Layout

- Header with hamburger + dropdown (user-pickable items).
- Hero logo centered.
- Footer single line: `© Copyright {year} HalloHome | Terms of Use | License`.

## Versioning

Carry per segment, base-10:

```
0.0.0 -> 0.0.1 -> ... -> 0.0.9 -> 0.1.0 -> ... -> 0.9.9 -> 1.0.0
```

Handled by `scripts/bump.mjs`. No alternative roadmap is permitted in code or UI.

## Verification

Every change must pass `pnpm verify`:

- RED -> fix -> re-verify.
- GREEN required before any release.
- Release via `pnpm bump` only after GREEN.

## Scripts

- `scripts/verify.mjs` — orchestrates all checks (exit 0 = GREEN, non-zero = RED).
- `scripts/bump.mjs` — full release pipeline (verify, version carry, manifests, README, restore, git add/commit/tag/push).
- `scripts/restore.mjs` — writes `scripts/restore.md`, single continuous file, overwritten each run, binary excluded.
- `scripts/logo.mjs` — generates brand PNGs into `src/ui/public/brand/`.

## Restore

`scripts/restore.md` is the single continuous snapshot of essential source files.

- Binary assets excluded (png, woff2, gltf, glb, etc.).
- Excluded directories: `node_modules`, `target`, `dist`, `build`, `.git`, `.svelte-kit`, `.vite`, `.cache`, `coverage`, `brand`.
- Overwritten every run.

## CI

`.github/workflows/verify.yml` runs on push and pull request to `main` and executes `pnpm verify`.
```

## `docs/USAGE.md`

```markdown
# HalloHome — Usage

## Prerequisites

- Node.js >= 20
- pnpm >= 9
- Rust >= 1.78 (with `rustfmt` and `clippy`)
- Linux system deps: `libgtk-3-dev`, `libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev`, `libappindicator3-dev`, `librsvg2-dev`, `patchelf`
- macOS / Windows: Tauri v2 prerequisites per https://v2.tauri.app/start/prerequisites/

## Install

```bash
git clone https://github.com/ramaekayudha/HalloHome.git
cd HalloHome
pnpm install
```

This generates `pnpm-lock.yaml`. The first `cargo build` generates `Cargo.lock`. Both must be committed.

## Fonts — MANDATORY

**HalloHome uses Lora only.** Before running `pnpm verify`, place these 4 files in `src/ui/public/fonts/`:

- `Lora-Regular.woff2`
- `Lora-Medium.woff2`
- `Lora-Bold.woff2`
- `Lora-Italic.woff2`

Full instructions (including download and conversion): see `src/ui/public/fonts/README.md`.

`pnpm verify` will fail if any of the 4 files are missing.

## Verify (mandatory before release)

```bash
pnpm verify
```

Runs in order:

0. Font presence check (4/4 required)
1. `pnpm logo` — generates brand PNGs and Tauri icons
2. `pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui build` — frontend dist
3. `cargo fmt --all -- --check`
4. `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`
5. `cargo test --workspace`
6. `cargo build --release --workspace`
7. `pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui typecheck`
8. `pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui lint`

All must be GREEN. Any failure must be fixed before release.

## Run in development

```bash
pnpm tauri dev
```

Launches Vite + Tauri shell.

## Manual test (must be GREEN before release)

- App window opens with title "HalloHome".
- Hero logo centered.
- Footer shows exactly one line: `© Copyright {year} HalloHome | Terms of Use | License`.
- Theme toggle switches light/dark and persists across restart.
- Hamburger opens dropdown; items are user-pickable.
- All text uses Lora only (DevTools → Computed → font-family contains `Lora`).
- Brand PNGs present in `src/ui/public/brand/`.

## Release

```bash
pnpm bump
```

Runs `pnpm verify`, computes next version (carry base-10 per segment), updates all manifests and README badge, regenerates `scripts/restore.md`, commits, tags, and pushes.

Version carry: `0.0.9 -> 0.1.0`, `0.9.9 -> 1.0.0`.

## Restore snapshot

```bash
pnpm restore
```

Regenerates `scripts/restore.md` — full source snapshot of essential files. Binary assets are excluded. Overwritten on each run.

## Brand assets

```bash
pnpm logo
```

Writes PNGs into:

- `src/ui/public/brand/` — favicon, apple-touch, hero logo
- `src/tauri/icons/` — Tauri app icons (including `icon.ico`)

## Lockfiles — must be committed

- `Cargo.lock`
- `pnpm-lock.yaml`

## Excluded from git

- `target/`, `node_modules/`, `dist/`, `build/`
- `src/ui/public/brand/` (regenerated by `pnpm logo`)
- `src/tauri/icons/` (regenerated by `pnpm logo`)
- `src/tauri/gen/` (regenerated by `tauri-build`)
- `.env*`, logs, OS files
```

## `README.md`

```markdown
# HalloHome

![Version](https://img.shields.io/badge/version-0.0.2-blue)
![Status](https://img.shields.io/badge/status-active-success)
![Tech](https://img.shields.io/badge/tech-Rust%20%2B%20Tauri%20v2%20%2B%20Svelte%205-orange)
![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-GPL--3.0--or--later-blue)

**HalloHome** — Floor plan editor with professional 3D rendering, a curated asset library, and a professional layout system.

## Stack

| Layer | Tech | Path |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Core engine | Rust (UI-agnostic) | `src/core` |
| Bridge | Tauri v2 | `src/tauri` |
| UI | Svelte 5 + Vite + TypeScript | `src/ui` |

- **Typography**: Lora only.
- **Themes**: Light (Corporate Blue) / Dark (Neon Orange). User-pickable.
- **3D assets**: glTF 2.0 / GLB.
- **Project file**: `.hallohome` (versioned JSON).

## Commands

| Command | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `pnpm verify` | Full verification (logo, UI build, fmt, clippy, test, build, typecheck, lint) |
| `pnpm bump` | Release: verify → carry version → update badges/manifests → restore → commit → tag → push |
| `pnpm restore` | Regenerate `scripts/restore.md` (source snapshot, binary excluded) |
| `pnpm logo` | Generate brand PNGs into `src/ui/public/brand/` |

## License

GPL-3.0-or-later. See [LICENSE](./LICENSE).
```

## `scripts/bump.mjs`

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * HalloHome — 100% release pipeline.
 *
 * Steps:
 *  1. Verify (must be GREEN).
 *  2. Compute next version (carry base-10 per segment).
 *  3. Update versions in: package.json (root + UI), Cargo.toml, tauri.conf.json, README badge.
 *  4. Regenerate scripts/restore.md.
 *  5. git add + commit + tag + push.
 */

import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = process.cwd();

function run(cmd, opts = {}) {
  console.log(`\n▶ ${cmd}`);
  execSync(cmd, { stdio: 'inherit', cwd: ROOT, ...opts });
}

function nextVersion(v) {
  const [maj, min, pat] = v.split('.').map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10));
  if (!Number.isFinite(maj) || !Number.isFinite(min) || !Number.isFinite(pat)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid version: ${v}`);
  }
  if (pat < 9) return `${maj}.${min}.${pat + 1}`;
  if (min < 9) return `${maj}.${min + 1}.0`;
  return `${maj + 1}.0.0`;
}

function readPkg(file) {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8'));
}

function writePkg(file, data) {
  fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n');
}

function replaceInFile(file, pattern, replacement) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return;
  const content = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  const next = content.replace(pattern, replacement);
  if (next === content) return;
  fs.writeFileSync(file, next);
}

// --- 0. Sanity ---
try {
  execSync('git rev-parse --git-dir', { stdio: 'ignore', cwd: ROOT });
} catch {
  console.error('❌ Not a git repository. Run `git init` first.');
  process.exit(1);
}

// --- 1. Verify ---
run('pnpm verify');

// --- 2. Compute next version ---
const rootPkgPath = path.join(ROOT, 'package.json');
const rootPkg = readPkg(rootPkgPath);
const current = rootPkg.version;
const next = nextVersion(current);
console.log(`\n📦 Version: ${current} → ${next}`);

// --- 3. Update versions ---
// Root package.json
rootPkg.version = next;
writePkg(rootPkgPath, rootPkg);

// UI package.json
const uiPkgPath = path.join(ROOT, 'src/ui/package.json');
if (fs.existsSync(uiPkgPath)) {
  const uiPkg = readPkg(uiPkgPath);
  uiPkg.version = next;
  writePkg(uiPkgPath, uiPkg);
}

// Workspace Cargo.toml
replaceInFile(
  path.join(ROOT, 'Cargo.toml'),
  /(\[workspace\.package\][\s\S]*?version\s*=\s*)"[^"]+"/,
  `$1"${next}"`
);

// tauri.conf.json
replaceInFile(
  path.join(ROOT, 'src/tauri/tauri.conf.json'),
  /"version"\s*:\s*"[^"]+"/,
  `"version": "${next}"`
);

// README badge
replaceInFile(
  path.join(ROOT, 'README.md'),
  /(badge\/version-)\d+\.\d+\.\d+(-)/g,
  `$1${next}$2`
);

// --- 4. Regenerate restore.md ---
run('node scripts/restore.mjs');

// --- 5. Commit, tag, push ---
run('git add -A');
run(`git commit -m "release: v${next}"`);
run(`git tag v${next}`);
run('git push origin HEAD --follow-tags');

console.log(`\n🚀 Released v${next}`);
```

## `scripts/logo.mjs`

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * HalloHome — brand + Tauri icon generator.
 *
 * Outputs:
 *   src/ui/public/brand/
 *     - favicon-32.png
 *     - apple-touch-180.png
 *     - logo-hero-512.png
 *     - logo.png
 *
 *   src/tauri/icons/         (required by tauri-build on all platforms)
 *     - 32x32.png
 *     - 128x128.png
 *     - 128x128@2x.png       (256x256)
 *     - icon.png             (512x512)
 *     - icon.ico             (256x256 PNG-wrapped ICO, Windows resource)
 */

import sharp from 'sharp';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const UI_OUT = path.join(ROOT, 'src/ui/public/brand');
const TAURI_OUT = path.join(ROOT, 'src/tauri/icons');

fs.mkdirSync(UI_OUT, { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(TAURI_OUT, { recursive: true });

const SIZE = 512;
const svg = `
<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${SIZE}" height="${SIZE}" viewBox="0 0 ${SIZE} ${SIZE}">
  <defs>
    <linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0%" stop-color="#1e40af"/>
      <stop offset="100%" stop-color="#ff7a00"/>
    </linearGradient>
  </defs>
  <rect width="${SIZE}" height="${SIZE}" rx="96" fill="url(#g)"/>
  <text x="50%" y="50%" text-anchor="middle" dominant-baseline="central"
        font-family="serif" font-size="280" font-weight="bold" fill="#ffffff">H</text>
</svg>`;

const buf = Buffer.from(svg);

// --- UI brand ---------------------------------------------------------
await sharp(buf).resize(32, 32).png().toFile(path.join(UI_OUT, 'favicon-32.png'));
await sharp(buf).resize(180, 180).png().toFile(path.join(UI_OUT, 'apple-touch-180.png'));
await sharp(buf).resize(512, 512).png().toFile(path.join(UI_OUT, 'logo-hero-512.png'));
await sharp(buf).resize(512, 512).png().toFile(path.join(UI_OUT, 'logo.png'));

// --- Tauri icons ------------------------------------------------------
await sharp(buf).resize(32, 32).png().toFile(path.join(TAURI_OUT, '32x32.png'));
await sharp(buf).resize(128, 128).png().toFile(path.join(TAURI_OUT, '128x128.png'));
await sharp(buf).resize(256, 256).png().toFile(path.join(TAURI_OUT, '128x128@2x.png'));
await sharp(buf).resize(512, 512).png().toFile(path.join(TAURI_OUT, 'icon.png'));

// icon.ico: single 256x256 PNG-wrapped ICO (Windows resource)
const png256 = await sharp(buf).resize(256, 256).png().toBuffer();
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(TAURI_OUT, 'icon.ico'), makeIco(png256));

/**
 * Wrap a single PNG buffer into a minimal ICO container.
 * Spec: ICONDIR (6 B) + ICONDIRENTRY (16 B) + PNG data.
 * Width/Height byte 0 means 256.
 */
function makeIco(pngBuf) {
  const header = Buffer.alloc(6);
  header.writeUInt16LE(0, 0); // reserved
  header.writeUInt16LE(1, 2); // type = 1 (ICO)
  header.writeUInt16LE(1, 4); // count = 1

  const entry = Buffer.alloc(16);
  entry.writeUInt8(0, 0); // width (0 = 256)
  entry.writeUInt8(0, 1); // height (0 = 256)
  entry.writeUInt8(0, 2); // color count
  entry.writeUInt8(0, 3); // reserved
  entry.writeUInt16LE(1, 4); // planes
  entry.writeUInt16LE(32, 6); // bit count
  entry.writeUInt32LE(pngBuf.length, 8); // size
  entry.writeUInt32LE(6 + 16, 12); // offset = 22

  return Buffer.concat([header, entry, pngBuf]);
}

console.log('✅ brand + Tauri icons generated');
```

## `scripts/restore.mjs`

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * HalloHome — restore snapshot generator.
 *
 * Output: scripts/restore.md (single continuous file, overwritten each run).
 * Only essential source files are recorded.
 * Binary assets (png, woff2, gltf, etc.) are EXCLUDED.
 * Excluded directories: node_modules, target, dist, .git, build, .svelte-kit.
 */

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const OUT = path.join(ROOT, 'scripts/restore.md');

const EXCLUDE_DIRS = new Set([
  'node_modules',
  'target',
  'dist',
  'build',
  '.git',
  '.svelte-kit',
  '.vite',
  '.cache',
  'coverage',
  'brand', // generated PNGs
]);

const EXCLUDE_FILES = new Set([
  'pnpm-lock.yaml',
  'Cargo.lock',
  'scripts/restore.md',
  '.DS_Store',
]);

const EXCLUDE_EXT = new Set([
  '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.ico', '.webp', '.svg',
  '.woff', '.woff2', '.ttf', '.otf', '.eot',
  '.gltf', '.glb', '.bin',
  '.mp3', '.mp4', '.wav',
  '.zip', '.tar', '.gz', '.7z',
]);

const LANG = {
  '.rs': 'rust',
  '.toml': 'toml',
  '.json': 'json',
  '.js': 'javascript',
  '.mjs': 'javascript',
  '.cjs': 'javascript',
  '.ts': 'typescript',
  '.svelte': 'svelte',
  '.css': 'css',
  '.html': 'html',
  '.md': 'markdown',
  '.yml': 'yaml',
  '.yaml': 'yaml',
  '.txt': 'text',
  '.sh': 'bash',
};

function shouldInclude(relPath, name) {
  if (EXCLUDE_FILES.has(relPath)) return false;
  const ext = path.extname(name).toLowerCase();
  if (EXCLUDE_EXT.has(ext)) return false;
  return true;
}

function walk(dir, prefix = '') {
  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
  const files = [];
  for (const entry of entries) {
    const relPath = prefix ? `${prefix}/${entry.name}` : entry.name;
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      if (EXCLUDE_DIRS.has(entry.name)) continue;
      files.push(...walk(fullPath, relPath));
    } else if (shouldInclude(relPath, entry.name)) {
      files.push({ relPath, fullPath });
    }
  }
  return files;
}

const allFiles = walk(ROOT).sort((a, b) => a.relPath.localeCompare(b.relPath));

let out = '# HalloHome — Restore Snapshot\n\n';
out += `Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}\n\n`;
out += `Total files: ${allFiles.length}\n\n`;
out += '---\n\n';

for (const { relPath, fullPath } of allFiles) {
  const content = fs.readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf8');
  const lang = LANG[path.extname(relPath).toLowerCase()] || 'text';
  out += `## \`${relPath}\`\n\n`;
  out += '```' + lang + '\n';
  out += content;
  if (!content.endsWith('\n')) out += '\n';
  out += '```\n\n';
}

fs.writeFileSync(OUT, out);
console.log(`✅ restore.md written: ${allFiles.length} files`);
```

## `scripts/verify.mjs`

```javascript
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * HalloHome — full verification.
 *
 * Order matters:
 *   0. Woff2 fonts must exist (4 files) — enforces "Lora only".
 *   1. Logo generation.
 *   2. Frontend build.
 *   3. Rust fmt / clippy / test / build.
 *   4. Frontend typecheck / lint.
 *
 * Exit code 0 = GREEN. Any failure = RED.
 */

import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = process.cwd();

function run(cmd) {
  console.log(`\n▶ ${cmd}`);
  execSync(cmd, { stdio: 'inherit', cwd: ROOT });
}

function checkFonts() {
  const dir = path.join(ROOT, 'src/ui/public/fonts');
  const required = [
    'Lora-Regular.woff2',
    'Lora-Medium.woff2',
    'Lora-Bold.woff2',
    'Lora-Italic.woff2',
  ];
  const missing = required.filter((f) => !fs.existsSync(path.join(dir, f)));
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    console.error('\n❌ MISSING LORA FONTS:');
    for (const f of missing) console.error(`   - src/ui/public/fonts/${f}`);
    console.error(
      '\n   See src/ui/public/fonts/README.md for download instructions.'
    );
    console.error('   Rule: only Lora is allowed. No fallback to other fonts.\n');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  console.log('✅ Lora fonts present (4/4)');
}

try {
  checkFonts();
  run('pnpm logo');
  run('pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui build');
  run('cargo fmt --all -- --check');
  run('cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings');
  run('cargo test --workspace');
  run('cargo build --release --workspace');
  run('pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui typecheck');
  run('pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui lint');
  console.log('\n✅ VERIFY PASSED — GREEN');
} catch {
  console.error('\n❌ VERIFY FAILED — RED');
  process.exit(1);
}
```

//! Asset management: glTF/GLB loading, catalog, thumbnails.

/// Canonical module name.
pub const MODULE_NAME: &str = "assets";

/// Canonical glTF extension.
pub const PRIMARY_MODEL_EXTENSION: &str = "gltf";
pub const PRIMARY_MODEL_EXTENSION_BINARY: &str = "glb";

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn module_name_ok() {
        assert_eq!(MODULE_NAME, "assets");
    }

    #[test]
    fn model_extensions_ok() {
        assert_eq!(PRIMARY_MODEL_EXTENSION, "gltf");
        assert_eq!(PRIMARY_MODEL_EXTENSION_BINARY, "glb");
    }
}

## `src/core/src/geometry/mod.rs`

```rust
//! Geometry primitives and transforms.

/// A 2D point in scene units.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Point2 {
    pub x: f32,
    pub y: f32,
}

impl Point2 {
    pub const fn new(x: f32, y: f32) -> Self {
        Self { x, y }
    }
}

/// A 3D point in scene units.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Point3 {
    pub x: f32,
    pub y: f32,
    pub z: f32,
}

impl Point3 {
    pub const fn new(x: f32, y: f32, z: f32) -> Self {
        Self { x, y, z }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn point2_constructs() {
        let p = Point2::new(1.0, 2.0);
        assert_eq!((p.x, p.y), (1.0, 2.0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn point3_constructs() {
        let p = Point3::new(1.0, 2.0, 3.0);
        assert_eq!((p.x, p.y, p.z), (1.0, 2.0, 3.0));
    }
}
```

## `src/core/src/lib.rs`

```rust
//! HalloHome core engine.
//!
//! UI-agnostic by contract. This crate MUST NOT import anything from
//! `src/ui` or `src/tauri`. Enforced by review and CI.

pub mod assets;
pub mod floorplan;
pub mod geometry;
pub mod project;
pub mod render3d;

/// Current core engine version, sourced from `Cargo.toml`.
pub fn version() -> &'static str {
    env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn version_is_not_empty() {
        assert!(!version().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn version_is_semver_like() {
        let v = version();
        let parts: Vec<&str> = v.split('.').collect();
        assert_eq!(parts.len(), 3, "version must have 3 segments: {v}");
        for p in parts {
            assert!(p.parse::<u32>().is_ok(), "non-numeric segment: {p}");
        }
    }
}
```

## `src/core/src/project/mod.rs`

//! Project file: `.hallohome` schema, serialization, migration.

/// Canonical module name.
pub const MODULE_NAME: &str = "project";

/// Canonical project file extension (without dot).
pub const FILE_EXTENSION: &str = "hallohome";

/// Current schema version of the `.hallohome` file format.
pub const SCHEMA_VERSION: u32 = 1;

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn module_name_ok() {
        assert_eq!(MODULE_NAME, "project");
    }

    #[test]
    fn file_extension_ok() {
        assert_eq!(FILE_EXTENSION, "hallohome");
    }

    #[test]
    fn schema_version_positive() {
        const { assert!(SCHEMA_VERSION >= 1) };
    }
}
```

## `src/tauri/gen/schemas/desktop-schema.json`

```json
{
  "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#",
  "title": "CapabilityFile",
  "description": "Capability formats accepted in a capability file.",
  "anyOf": [
    {
      "description": "A single capability.",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/Capability"
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "description": "A list of capabilities.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Capability"
      }
    },
    {
      "description": "A list of capabilities.",
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "capabilities"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "capabilities": {
          "description": "The list of capabilities.",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/Capability"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ],
  "definitions": {
    "Capability": {
      "description": "A grouping and boundary mechanism developers can use to isolate access to the IPC layer.\n\nIt controls application windows' and webviews' fine grained access to the Tauri core, application, or plugin commands. If a webview or its window is not matching any capability then it has no access to the IPC layer at all.\n\nThis can be done to create groups of windows, based on their required system access, which can reduce impact of frontend vulnerabilities in less privileged windows. Windows can be added to a capability by exact name (e.g. `main-window`) or glob patterns like `*` or `admin-*`. A Window can have none, one, or multiple associated capabilities.\n\n## Example\n\n```json { \"identifier\": \"main-user-files-write\", \"description\": \"This capability allows the `main` window on macOS and Windows access to `filesystem` write related commands and `dialog` commands to enable programmatic access to files selected by the user.\", \"windows\": [ \"main\" ], \"permissions\": [ \"core:default\", \"dialog:open\", { \"identifier\": \"fs:allow-write-text-file\", \"allow\": [{ \"path\": \"$HOME/test.txt\" }] }, ], \"platforms\": [\"macOS\",\"windows\"] } ```",
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "identifier",
        "permissions"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "identifier": {
          "description": "Identifier of the capability.\n\n## Example\n\n`main-user-files-write`",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "description": {
          "description": "Description of what the capability is intended to allow on associated windows.\n\nIt should contain a description of what the grouped permissions should allow.\n\n## Example\n\nThis capability allows the `main` window access to `filesystem` write related commands and `dialog` commands to enable programmatic access to files selected by the user.",
          "default": "",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "remote": {
          "description": "Configure remote URLs that can use the capability permissions.\n\nThis setting is optional and defaults to not being set, as our default use case is that the content is served from our local application.\n\n:::caution Make sure you understand the security implications of providing remote sources with local system access. :::\n\n## Example\n\n```json { \"urls\": [\"https://*.mydomain.dev\"] } ```",
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/definitions/CapabilityRemote"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "local": {
          "description": "Whether this capability is enabled for local app URLs or not. Defaults to `true`.",
          "default": true,
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "windows": {
          "description": "List of windows that are affected by this capability. Can be a glob pattern.\n\nIf a window label matches any of the patterns in this list, the capability will be enabled on all the webviews of that window, regardless of the value of [`Self::webviews`].\n\nOn multiwebview windows, prefer specifying [`Self::webviews`] and omitting [`Self::windows`] for a fine grained access control.\n\n## Example\n\n`[\"main\"]`",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "webviews": {
          "description": "List of webviews that are affected by this capability. Can be a glob pattern.\n\nThe capability will be enabled on all the webviews whose label matches any of the patterns in this list, regardless of whether the webview's window label matches a pattern in [`Self::windows`].\n\n## Example\n\n`[\"sub-webview-one\", \"sub-webview-two\"]`",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "permissions": {
          "description": "List of permissions attached to this capability.\n\nMust include the plugin name as prefix in the form of `${plugin-name}:${permission-name}`. For commands directly implemented in the application itself only `${permission-name}` is required.\n\n## Example\n\n```json [ \"core:default\", \"shell:allow-open\", \"dialog:open\", { \"identifier\": \"fs:allow-write-text-file\", \"allow\": [{ \"path\": \"$HOME/test.txt\" }] } ] ```",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/PermissionEntry"
          },
          "uniqueItems": true
        },
        "platforms": {
          "description": "Limit which target platforms this capability applies to.\n\nBy default all platforms are targeted.\n\n## Example\n\n`[\"macOS\",\"windows\"]`",
          "type": [
            "array",
            "null"
          ],
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/Target"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "CapabilityRemote": {
      "description": "Configuration for remote URLs that are associated with the capability.",
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "urls"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "urls": {
          "description": "Remote domains this capability refers to using the [URLPattern standard](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/).\n\n## Examples\n\n- \"https://*.mydomain.dev\": allows subdomains of mydomain.dev - \"https://mydomain.dev/api/*\": allows any subpath of mydomain.dev/api",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "PermissionEntry": {
      "description": "An entry for a permission value in a [`Capability`] can be either a raw permission [`Identifier`] or an object that references a permission and extends its scope.",
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "description": "Reference a permission or permission set by identifier.",
          "allOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/definitions/Identifier"
            }
          ]
        },
        {
          "description": "Reference a permission or permission set by identifier and extends its scope.",
          "type": "object",
          "allOf": [
            {
              "properties": {
                "identifier": {
                  "description": "Identifier of the permission or permission set.",
                  "allOf": [
                    {
                      "$ref": "#/definitions/Identifier"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "allow": {
                  "description": "Data that defines what is allowed by the scope.",
                  "type": [
                    "array",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "items": {
                    "$ref": "#/definitions/Value"
                  }
                },
                "deny": {
                  "description": "Data that defines what is denied by the scope. This should be prioritized by validation logic.",
                  "type": [
                    "array",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "items": {
                    "$ref": "#/definitions/Value"
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ],
          "required": [
            "identifier"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "Identifier": {
      "description": "Permission identifier",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "description": "Default core plugins set.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `core:path:default`\n- `core:event:default`\n- `core:window:default`\n- `core:webview:default`\n- `core:app:default`\n- `core:image:default`\n- `core:resources:default`\n- `core:menu:default`\n- `core:tray:default`",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:default",
          "markdownDescription": "Default core plugins set.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `core:path:default`\n- `core:event:default`\n- `core:window:default`\n- `core:webview:default`\n- `core:app:default`\n- `core:image:default`\n- `core:resources:default`\n- `core:menu:default`\n- `core:tray:default`"
        },
        {
          "description": "Default permissions for the plugin.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `allow-version`\n- `allow-name`\n- `allow-tauri-version`\n- `allow-identifier`\n- `allow-bundle-type`\n- `allow-register-listener`\n- `allow-remove-listener`\n- `allow-supports-multiple-windows`",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:default",
          "markdownDescription": "Default permissions for the plugin.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `allow-version`\n- `allow-name`\n- `allow-tauri-version`\n- `allow-identifier`\n- `allow-bundle-type`\n- `allow-register-listener`\n- `allow-remove-listener`\n- `allow-supports-multiple-windows`"
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the app_hide command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-app-hide",
          "markdownDescription": "Enables the app_hide command without any pre-configured scope."
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the app_show command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-app-show",
          "markdownDescription": "Enables the app_show command without any pre-configured scope."
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the bundle_type command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-bundle-type",

        },
        {

          "type": "string",

## `src/tauri/gen/schemas/windows-schema.json`

```json
{
  "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#",
  "title": "CapabilityFile",
  "description": "Capability formats accepted in a capability file.",
  "anyOf": [
    {
      "description": "A single capability.",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/Capability"
        }
      ]
    },
    {
      "description": "A list of capabilities.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Capability"
      }
    },
    {
      "description": "A list of capabilities.",
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "capabilities"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "capabilities": {
          "description": "The list of capabilities.",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/Capability"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  ],
  "definitions": {
    "Capability": {
      "description": "A grouping and boundary mechanism developers can use to isolate access to the IPC layer.\n\nIt controls application windows' and webviews' fine grained access to the Tauri core, application, or plugin commands. If a webview or its window is not matching any capability then it has no access to the IPC layer at all.\n\nThis can be done to create groups of windows, based on their required system access, which can reduce impact of frontend vulnerabilities in less privileged windows. Windows can be added to a capability by exact name (e.g. `main-window`) or glob patterns like `*` or `admin-*`. A Window can have none, one, or multiple associated capabilities.\n\n## Example\n\n```json { \"identifier\": \"main-user-files-write\", \"description\": \"This capability allows the `main` window on macOS and Windows access to `filesystem` write related commands and `dialog` commands to enable programmatic access to files selected by the user.\", \"windows\": [ \"main\" ], \"permissions\": [ \"core:default\", \"dialog:open\", { \"identifier\": \"fs:allow-write-text-file\", \"allow\": [{ \"path\": \"$HOME/test.txt\" }] }, ], \"platforms\": [\"macOS\",\"windows\"] } ```",
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "identifier",
        "permissions"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "identifier": {
          "description": "Identifier of the capability.\n\n## Example\n\n`main-user-files-write`",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "description": {
          "description": "Description of what the capability is intended to allow on associated windows.\n\nIt should contain a description of what the grouped permissions should allow.\n\n## Example\n\nThis capability allows the `main` window access to `filesystem` write related commands and `dialog` commands to enable programmatic access to files selected by the user.",
          "default": "",
          "type": "string"
        },
        "remote": {
          "description": "Configure remote URLs that can use the capability permissions.\n\nThis setting is optional and defaults to not being set, as our default use case is that the content is served from our local application.\n\n:::caution Make sure you understand the security implications of providing remote sources with local system access. :::\n\n## Example\n\n```json { \"urls\": [\"https://*.mydomain.dev\"] } ```",
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/definitions/CapabilityRemote"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "local": {
          "description": "Whether this capability is enabled for local app URLs or not. Defaults to `true`.",
          "default": true,
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "windows": {
          "description": "List of windows that are affected by this capability. Can be a glob pattern.\n\nIf a window label matches any of the patterns in this list, the capability will be enabled on all the webviews of that window, regardless of the value of [`Self::webviews`].\n\nOn multiwebview windows, prefer specifying [`Self::webviews`] and omitting [`Self::windows`] for a fine grained access control.\n\n## Example\n\n`[\"main\"]`",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "webviews": {
          "description": "List of webviews that are affected by this capability. Can be a glob pattern.\n\nThe capability will be enabled on all the webviews whose label matches any of the patterns in this list, regardless of whether the webview's window label matches a pattern in [`Self::windows`].\n\n## Example\n\n`[\"sub-webview-one\", \"sub-webview-two\"]`",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "permissions": {
          "description": "List of permissions attached to this capability.\n\nMust include the plugin name as prefix in the form of `${plugin-name}:${permission-name}`. For commands directly implemented in the application itself only `${permission-name}` is required.\n\n## Example\n\n```json [ \"core:default\", \"shell:allow-open\", \"dialog:open\", { \"identifier\": \"fs:allow-write-text-file\", \"allow\": [{ \"path\": \"$HOME/test.txt\" }] } ] ```",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/PermissionEntry"
          },
          "uniqueItems": true
        },
        "platforms": {
          "description": "Limit which target platforms this capability applies to.\n\nBy default all platforms are targeted.\n\n## Example\n\n`[\"macOS\",\"windows\"]`",
          "type": [
            "array",
            "null"
          ],
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/Target"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "CapabilityRemote": {
      "description": "Configuration for remote URLs that are associated with the capability.",
      "type": "object",
      "required": [
        "urls"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "urls": {
          "description": "Remote domains this capability refers to using the [URLPattern standard](https://urlpattern.spec.whatwg.org/).\n\n## Examples\n\n- \"https://*.mydomain.dev\": allows subdomains of mydomain.dev - \"https://mydomain.dev/api/*\": allows any subpath of mydomain.dev/api",
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "PermissionEntry": {
      "description": "An entry for a permission value in a [`Capability`] can be either a raw permission [`Identifier`] or an object that references a permission and extends its scope.",
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "description": "Reference a permission or permission set by identifier.",
          "allOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/definitions/Identifier"
            }
          ]
        },
        {
          "description": "Reference a permission or permission set by identifier and extends its scope.",
          "type": "object",
          "allOf": [
            {
              "properties": {
                "identifier": {
                  "description": "Identifier of the permission or permission set.",
                  "allOf": [
                    {
                      "$ref": "#/definitions/Identifier"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "allow": {
                  "description": "Data that defines what is allowed by the scope.",
                  "type": [
                    "array",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "items": {
                    "$ref": "#/definitions/Value"
                  }
                },
                "deny": {
                  "description": "Data that defines what is denied by the scope. This should be prioritized by validation logic.",
                  "type": [
                    "array",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "items": {
                    "$ref": "#/definitions/Value"
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          ],
          "required": [
            "identifier"
          ]
        }
      ]
    },
    "Identifier": {
      "description": "Permission identifier",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "description": "Default core plugins set.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `core:path:default`\n- `core:event:default`\n- `core:window:default`\n- `core:webview:default`\n- `core:app:default`\n- `core:image:default`\n- `core:resources:default`\n- `core:menu:default`\n- `core:tray:default`",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:default",
          "markdownDescription": "Default core plugins set.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `core:path:default`\n- `core:event:default`\n- `core:window:default`\n- `core:webview:default`\n- `core:app:default`\n- `core:image:default`\n- `core:resources:default`\n- `core:menu:default`\n- `core:tray:default`"
        },
        {
          "description": "Default permissions for the plugin.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `allow-version`\n- `allow-name`\n- `allow-tauri-version`\n- `allow-identifier`\n- `allow-bundle-type`\n- `allow-register-listener`\n- `allow-remove-listener`\n- `allow-supports-multiple-windows`",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:default",
          "markdownDescription": "Default permissions for the plugin.\n#### This default permission set includes:\n\n- `allow-version`\n- `allow-name`\n- `allow-tauri-version`\n- `allow-identifier`\n- `allow-bundle-type`\n- `allow-register-listener`\n- `allow-remove-listener`\n- `allow-supports-multiple-windows`"
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the app_hide command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-app-hide",
          "markdownDescription": "Enables the app_hide command without any pre-configured scope."
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the app_show command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-app-show",
          "markdownDescription": "Enables the app_show command without any pre-configured scope."
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the bundle_type command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-bundle-type",
          "markdownDescription": "Enables the bundle_type command without any pre-configured scope."
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the default_window_icon command without any pre-configured scope.",
          "type": "string",
          "const": "core:app:allow-default-window-icon",
          "markdownDescription": "Enables the default_window_icon command without any pre-configured scope."
        },
        {
          "description": "Enables the exit command without any pre-configured scope.",

## `src/tauri/src/main.rs`

```rust
//! HalloHome desktop app entry — Tauri v2 bridge.
//!
//! The bridge is a thin shell: it owns the window and forwards commands
//! to `hallohome-core`. No business logic belongs here.

#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]

/// Returns the core engine version to the UI.
#[tauri::command]
fn core_version() -> &'static str {
    hallohome_core::version()
}

fn main() {
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![core_version])
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while running HalloHome");
}
```

## `src/tauri/tauri.conf.json`

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://schema.tauri.app/config/2",
  "productName": "HalloHome",
  "version": "0.0.2",
  "identifier": "com.hallohome.app",
  "build": {
    "frontendDist": "../../src/ui/dist",
    "beforeBuildCommand": "pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui build",
    "beforeDevCommand": "pnpm --filter @hallohome/ui dev",
    "devUrl": "http://localhost:5173"
  },
  "app": {
    "windows": [
      {
        "title": "HalloHome",
        "width": 1280,
        "height": 800,
        "resizable": true,
        "center": true
      }
    ]
  },
  "bundle": {
    "active": false,
    "icon": [
      "icons/32x32.png",
      "icons/128x128.png",
      "icons/128x128@2x.png",
      "icons/icon.ico",
      "icons/icon.png"
    ]
  }
}
```

## `src/ui/eslint.config.js`

```javascript
import js from '@eslint/js';
import tseslint from 'typescript-eslint';
import svelte from 'eslint-plugin-svelte';
import globals from 'globals';

export default tseslint.config(
  {
    ignores: ['dist/', 'node_modules/', '.svelte-kit/', 'public/brand/'],
  },
  js.configs.recommended,
  ...tseslint.configs.recommended,
  ...svelte.configs['flat/recommended'],
  {
    languageOptions: {
      globals: {
        ...globals.browser,
      },
    },
  },
  {
    files: ['**/*.svelte'],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        parser: tseslint.parser,
        extraFileExtensions: ['.svelte'],
      },
    },
  }
);
```

## `src/ui/public/fonts/README.md`

```markdown
# Lora Fonts — Required

**Rule: HalloHome uses Lora only. No other fonts are permitted.**

Place these files in this folder (`src/ui/public/fonts/`):

- `Lora-Regular.woff2`
- `Lora-Medium.woff2`
- `Lora-Bold.woff2`
- `Lora-Italic.woff2`

`pnpm verify` will fail if any of them is missing.

## How to obtain

1. Open https://fonts.google.com/specimen/Lora
2. Click **"Get font"** → **"Download all"**.
3. Unzip the download. It contains `static/` and `Lora-VariableFont_wght.ttf`.
4. Convert the following TTFs to `.woff2` (any online tool or `fonttools`):

   - `static/Lora-Regular.ttf` → `Lora-Regular.woff2`
   - `static/Lora-Medium.ttf` → `Lora-Medium.woff2`
   - `static/Lora-Bold.ttf` → `Lora-Bold.woff2`
   - `static/Lora-Italic.ttf` → `Lora-Italic.woff2`

   Using `fonttools`:

   ```
   pip install fonttools brotli
   fonttools ttLib.woff2 compress -o Lora-Regular.woff2 static/Lora-Regular.ttf
   ```

5. Drop the 4 `.woff2` files into this folder.

## License

Lora is licensed under the SIL Open Font License 1.1. The full text is in `OFL.txt` in this folder.
```

## `src/ui/src/App.svelte`

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import Header from './components/Header.svelte';
  import HeroLogo from './components/HeroLogo.svelte';
  import Footer from './components/Footer.svelte';

  type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';

  function initialTheme(): Theme {
    try {
      const s = localStorage.getItem('hallohome.theme');
      if (s === 'dark' || s === 'light') return s;
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
    return 'light';
  }

  let theme = $state<Theme>(initialTheme());

  $effect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('hallohome.theme', theme);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  });

  function toggleTheme() {
    theme = theme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light';
  }
</script>

<Header {theme} onToggleTheme={toggleTheme} />

<main class="main">
  <HeroLogo />
</main>

<Footer />

<style>
  .main {
    flex: 1;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    padding: 2rem;
  }
</style>
```

## `src/ui/src/components/Header.svelte`

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  import ThemeToggle from './ThemeToggle.svelte';

  let {
    theme,
    onToggleTheme,
  }: {
    theme: 'light' | 'dark';
    onToggleTheme: () => void;
  } = $props();

  let open = $state(false);

  const menuItems = ['Editor', 'Library', 'Templates', 'Settings'] as const;

  function toggle() {
    open = !open;
  }

  function close() {
    open = false;
  }
</script>

<header class="header">
  <div class="brand">
    <span class="brand-mark">H</span>
    <span class="brand-name">HalloHome</span>
  </div>

  <div class="actions">
    <ThemeToggle {theme} onToggle={onToggleTheme} />
    <button
      class="hamburger"
      aria-label="Toggle menu"
      aria-expanded={open}
      onclick={toggle}
    >
      <span></span>
      <span></span>
      <span></span>
    </button>
  </div>

  {#if open}
    <nav class="dropdown" aria-label="Main menu">
      {#each menuItems as item}
        <button class="dropdown-item" onclick={close}>
          {item}
        </button>
      {/each}
    </nav>
  {/if}
</header>

<style>
  .header {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    padding: 1rem 1.5rem;
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow);
  }

  .brand {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .brand-mark {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    width: 36px;
    height: 36px;
    border-radius: 10px;
    background: var(--accent);
    color: #fff;
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
  }

  .brand-name {
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: 1.1rem;
    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  }

  .actions {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }

  .hamburger {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 4px;
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    padding: 0 10px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
  }

  .hamburger span {
    display: block;
    height: 2px;
    background: var(--text);
    border-radius: 2px;
    transition:
      transform var(--transition),
      opacity var(--transition);
  }

  .hamburger[aria-expanded='true'] span:nth-child(1) {
    transform: translateY(6px) rotate(45deg);
  }
  .hamburger[aria-expanded='true'] span:nth-child(2) {
    opacity: 0;
  }
  .hamburger[aria-expanded='true'] span:nth-child(3) {
    transform: translateY(-6px) rotate(-45deg);
  }

  .dropdown {
    position: absolute;
    top: 100%;
    right: 1.5rem;
    min-width: 200px;
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    border-radius: 12px;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow);
    padding: 0.5rem;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 0.25rem;
    z-index: 10;
  }

  .dropdown-item {
    text-align: left;
    padding: 0.6rem 0.75rem;
    border-radius: 8px;
    color: var(--text);
    transition:
      background var(--transition),
      color var(--transition);
  }

  .dropdown-item:hover,
  .dropdown-item:focus-visible {
    background: var(--accent-soft);
    color: var(--accent);
    outline: none;
  }
</style>
```

## `src/ui/src/components/ThemeToggle.svelte`

```svelte
<script lang="ts">
  let {
    theme,
    onToggle,
  }: {
    theme: 'light' | 'dark';
    onToggle: () => void;
  } = $props();
</script>

<button
  class="theme-toggle"
  aria-label={`Switch to ${theme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light'} mode`}
  onclick={onToggle}
>
  {#if theme === 'light'}
    <svg
      width="20"
      height="20"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      stroke-width="2"
      stroke-linecap="round"
      stroke-linejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <path d="M21 12.79A9 9 0 1 1 11.21 3 7 7 0 0 0 21 12.79z" />
    </svg>
  {:else}
    <svg
      width="20"
      height="20"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      stroke-width="2"
      stroke-linecap="round"
      stroke-linejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4" />
      <path
        d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M4.93 4.93l1.41 1.41M17.66 17.66l1.41 1.41M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.93 19.07l1.41-1.41M17.66 6.34l1.41-1.41"
      />
    </svg>
  {/if}
</button>

<style>
  .theme-toggle {
    width: 40px;
    height: 40px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    border: 1px solid var(--border);
    background: var(--bg-elevated);
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    color: var(--text);
    transition:
      background var(--transition),
      border-color var(--transition),
      color var(--transition);
  }

  .theme-toggle:hover {
    border-color: var(--accent);
    color: var(--accent);
  }
</style>
```

## `src/ui/src/styles/theme.css`

```css
:root {
  --font-family: 'Lora', serif;
  --transition: 200ms ease;
}

:root[data-theme='light'] {
  --bg: #f5f7fb;
  --bg-elevated: #ffffff;
  --text: #0a1f44;
  --text-muted: #5b6b8a;
  --accent: #1e40af;
  --accent-soft: #dbe4ff;
  --border: #d7def0;
  --shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(10, 31, 68, 0.08);
}

:root[data-theme='dark'] {
  --bg: #0a0f1a;
  --bg-elevated: #121a2b;
  --text: #fefefe;
  --text-muted: #9ba9c4;
  --accent: #ff7a00;
  --accent-soft: #2a1a08;
  --border: #23314d;
  --shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(255, 122, 0, 0.15);
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html,
body {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
}

body {
  font-family: var(--font-family);
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--text);
  transition:
    background var(--transition),
    color var(--transition);
  min-height: 100vh;
}

#app {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

button {
  font-family: var(--font-family);
  cursor: pointer;
  border: none;
  background: none;
  color: inherit;
}
```
